Your reward = b * (number of cooperating neighbors) - c * (your number of neighbors if you cooperate, 0 if you defect). Your goal is to maximize your own cumulative reward over many rounds. Each round you will see how many of your neighbors cooperated and your own past actions and rewards. First, reason step-by-step about your decision inside <think>...</think> tags. Consider your history, your neighbors' behavior, and the payoff structure. Then, after the closing </think> tag, output ONLY the single digit 0 or 1.
